# Skills · M · part 47

790 entries, mass-spectrometry-processing-benchmark-timing to master-prompt-forge. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **mass-spectrometry-processing-benchmark-timing**: Use when a task asks to verify or reproduce the reported processing-speed benchmark for rtmsEcho or similar AEMS pipelines, e.g. timing a full 384-well plate full-scan (PI) run against the &lt;30 s (up to 60 Da window) claim. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/mass-spectrometry-processing-benchmark-timing))
- **mass-spectrometry-qc-criteria-definition**: Use when when setting up a new LC-MS QC workflow or modifying existing QC rules: you have access to internal standards and target analytes, know their expected retention times and m/z values, and need to establish pass/fail boundaries for sample acceptance before or concurrent with instrument runs. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/mass-spectrometry-qc-criteria-definition) · 2 copies)
- **mass-spectrometry-qc-metrics**: Use when evaluating Thermo Fisher RAW-file acquisition quality — e.g. verifying that MS2 scan fractions (e.g. 60%/62% reference vs 56%/59% underperforming) or scan-level TIC/injection-time trends match expected instrument behaviour. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/mass-spectrometry-qc-metrics))
- **mass-spectrometry-qc-plot-generation**: Use when peak metrics for auto-tracked MS1 ions or user-specified theoretical/reference ion targets have already been extracted and you need per-ion, per-metric CSV reports plus JPG/PDF figures to inspect and track instrument performance. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/mass-spectrometry-qc-plot-generation))
- **mass-spectrometry-qc-tabulation**: Use when vendor-agnostic QC of LC-HRMS acquisitions is needed: computing per-analyte retention time RSD, peak areas, or extracted-ion intensities from SST mixture mzML files to assess instrument performance before data collection. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/mass-spectrometry-qc-tabulation))
- **mass-spectrometry-quality-control-image-generation**: Use when performing batch-level quality assessment of untargeted LC-MS, LC-IMS-MS, or direct-infusion MS1 data (Agilent 'd', Thermo '.raw', Bruker 'd', or mzML) and you need a per-sample visual/PCA representation before extracting detailed peak metrics. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/mass-spectrometry-quality-control-image-generation))
- **mass-spectrometry-quality-metrics-extraction**: Use when you have centroided .mzML LC–MS data, a validated target compound list with adjusted expected retention times (e.g., after a screening mode run), and need to quantify peak quality and integration reliability across multiple sample runs to support metabolomics or lipidomics workflows. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/mass-spectrometry-quality-metrics-extraction) · 2 copies)
- **mass-spectrometry-quantification**: Use when you have measured MS intensity data from unknown samples and known-concentration standard compounds, and you need to convert sample intensities into predicted concentrations. Specifically applicable when intensity drift is observed across the measurement sequence (e. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/mass-spectrometry-quantification) · 2 copies)
- **mass-spectrometry-quantification-extraction**: Use when you have raw LipidSearch or LIQUID output files (CSV or TSV format) containing lipid identifiers and per-sample quantification measurements, and you need to convert them into a machine-readable data matrix for downstream statistical analysis, normalization, or differential abundance. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/mass-spectrometry-quantification-extraction) · 2 copies)
- **mass-spectrometry-query-formulation**: Use when you have a high-resolution LC-MS/MS experiment with a measured [M+H]+ or [M-H]− ion mass and optionally a parent ion fragmentation spectrum (peak list with m/z and intensity pairs), and you seek to generate candidate molecular structures for an unknown metabolite that may not be in. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/mass-spectrometry-query-formulation) · 3 copies)
- **mass-spectrometry-query-semantics**: Use when when you need to enable users to express complex mass spectrometry search patterns (e. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v1/skills/mass-spectrometry-query-semantics))
- **mass-spectrometry-query-semantics**: Use when when you need to express complex mass spectrometry search patterns (e.g., isotope envelope detection, neutral loss patterns, intensity relationships across m/z ranges) in a human-readable format that can be executed against spectral data files (mzML, mzXML) or spectral repositories. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/mass-spectrometry-query-semantics) · 2 copies)
- **mass-spectrometry-raw-data-encoding**: Use when you have generated or obtained a two-dimensional mass-spectrometry intensity matrix (m/z × retention time scan points) with simulated or experimental peak shapes, noise, and background, and need to encode it as a binary . ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/mass-spectrometry-raw-data-encoding) · 3 copies)
- **mass-spectrometry-raw-data-extraction**: Use when raw MS data stored in the MZA format (HDF5-based, converted from vendor .d/.raw/mzML via the mza converter) must be read into numpy arrays, especially multidimensional IMS-MS data. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/mass-spectrometry-raw-data-extraction))
- **mass-spectrometry-raw-data-preprocessing**: Use when when you have raw DIA mass spectrometry data files (.raw, .d, . ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/mass-spectrometry-raw-data-preprocessing) · 3 copies)
- **mass-spectrometry-raw-data-processing**: Use when the input is raw MS1-level LC/HRMS data (mzXML, mzML, or netCDF) from population-scale untargeted metabolomics/exposomics studies and you need a comprehensive, high-quality peak table of carbon-containing organic small molecules rather than alignment-ready region-of-interest output. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/mass-spectrometry-raw-data-processing))
- **mass-spectrometry-raw-file-conversion**: Use when newly acquired vendor-format raw files (e.g., Thermo .raw from an Orbitrap sequence) must be made pipeline-ready in open mzML format before MS-DIAL feature extraction and internal-standard QC. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/mass-spectrometry-raw-file-conversion))
- **mass-spectrometry-raw-file-handling**: Use when you have raw MS data files from Thermo Orbitrap or other vendor instruments that must be uploaded into a centralized platform for automated processing. Use it at the start of a multi-stage omics workflow where data collection, management, processing, and visualization are integrated; ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/mass-spectrometry-raw-file-handling) · 2 copies)
- **mass-spectrometry-record-serialization**: Use when input is a peak list or XML-based MS data file (DTA, MS2, MGF, PKL, mzXML, mzData, mzML, PRIDE XML) referenced by mzIdentML, and the goal is extracting per-spectrum records (id, CV parameters, peak lists) into structured JSON. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-proteomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-proteomics/tree/HEAD/collections/proteomics/v1/leaves/mass-spectrometry-record-serialization))
- **mass-spectrometry-reference-database-integration**: Use when you have individual MS/MS spectra or batch .mgf files from untargeted metabolomics experiments and need to search them against domain-specific reference libraries (e. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/mass-spectrometry-reference-database-integration) · 2 copies)
- **mass-spectrometry-reference-standard-alignment**: Use when you have positive- or negative-mode tunemix reference data (with known CCS values, m/z, and measured drift times) and need to establish a calibration model for converting observed drift times into CCS values for downstream feature annotation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/mass-spectrometry-reference-standard-alignment) · 3 copies)
- **mass-spectrometry-reference-standard-mapping**: Use when you have acquired tunemix data (positive or negative ion mode, in .h5 format) with known CCS reference values and need to construct a calibration function that will later predict CCS values for unknown analytes. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/mass-spectrometry-reference-standard-mapping) · 4 copies)
- **mass-spectrometry-representation-learning**: Use when raw MS vectors (e.g. TIC-normalized spectra with spectrum_dim 9900–15000) need a richer feature representation than a single-channel 1D vector, to improve classification accuracy and computational efficiency of CNN classifiers like ResNet50 or DenseNet121. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/mass-spectrometry-representation-learning))
- **mass-spectrometry-resolution-aware-peak-shape-modeling**: Use when a target metabolite's molecular formula and adduct are known and you need resolution-aware theoretical isotope patterns or tracer-isotopologue m/z windows as target lists for downstream quantification. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/mass-spectrometry-resolution-aware-peak-shape-modeling))
- **mass-spectrometry-result-tabulation**: Use when after executing a MassQL query against mzML mass spectrometry data files and obtaining tabulated results (DataFrame or equivalent in-memory table), apply this skill to persist those results in both human-readable CSV format and visual image form for archival, sharing, and downstream. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/mass-spectrometry-result-tabulation) · 2 copies)
- **mass-spectrometry-runtime-benchmarking**: Use when comparing runtime efficiency of mass spectrometry processing libraries on identical peak-processing steps (m/z trimming, precursor/noise removal, intensity filtering and scaling) over MGF spectra. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/mass-spectrometry-runtime-benchmarking))
- **mass-spectrometry-sample-traceability**: Use when preparing an injection worksheet for GC/LC-MS metabolomics/proteomics where QC samples must occupy a dedicated plate area, well positions must not interpenetrate experimental wells, and injection order must be traceable independently of well layout. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/mass-spectrometry-sample-traceability))
- **mass-spectrometry-scan-extraction-by-target**: Use when you have raw LC-MS/MS chromatogram files in mzML/mzXML format (converted from Thermo, Waters, or Bruker instruments) acquired in DDA or targeted MS/MS mode, and you need to isolate specific MS1 precursors and their corresponding MS2 fragments based on known m/z values and optional. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/mass-spectrometry-scan-extraction-by-target) · 2 copies)
- **mass-spectrometry-scan-indexing**: Use when you have a Thermo Fisher Scientific .raw file and need to (1) enumerate all scans and their metadata, (2) identify which scans are MS1 vs. MSn to enable level-specific filtering, (3) retrieve scan ranges or specific scan numbers for targeted spectral extraction, or (4) plan. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/mass-spectrometry-scan-indexing) · 2 copies)
- **mass-spectrometry-scoring-method-validation**: Use when you have high-resolution LC-MS data processed through both XCMS feature detection and RAMClustR clustering, and you need to verify the reliability of molecular weight assignments before downstream annotation or statistical analysis. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/mass-spectrometry-scoring-method-validation) · 3 copies)
- **mass-spectrometry-screening-workflows**: Use when when you have high-resolution LC-MS or GC-MS data from environmental samples and need to simultaneously screen for both known suspect chemicals and their transformation products, rather than targeting single compounds. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/mass-spectrometry-screening-workflows) · 4 copies)
- **mass-spectrometry-seasonality-analysis**: Use when a metabolomics dataset (e.g. mzML/mzXML from MassIVE MSV000097575 wastewater influent, n=55) has time-stamped samples and you need to quantify temporal drug-exposure trends from untargeted MS/MS annotations. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/mass-spectrometry-seasonality-analysis))
- **mass-spectrometry-signal-classification-evaluation**: Use when labeled synthetic mass-spectrometry windows (signal vs. noise peaks) are available and you need ROC-based comparison of a classification method against intensity-threshold filtering to justify a denoising prefilter. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/mass-spectrometry-signal-classification-evaluation))
- **mass-spectrometry-signal-processing**: Use when a 1D MS signal (e.g., an extracted ion chromatogram or arrival time distribution from MZA data) needs peak detection, interpolation, or peak-area quantification, and detections must be validated against known peak positions. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/mass-spectrometry-signal-processing))
- **mass-spectrometry-software-cli-execution**: Use when you need to reproduce or benchmark compareMS2's pairwise tandem mass spectrum comparisons from the CLI — e.g. timing the 10 pairwise comparisons of five serum datasets under default parameters — rather than using the GUI. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/mass-spectrometry-software-cli-execution))
- **mass-spectrometry-spectral-alignment**: Use when you have a pair of MS/MS spectra—one from a known compound and one from a structurally modified variant of that compound—and you need to identify which atoms in the structure likely bear the modification. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/mass-spectrometry-spectral-alignment) · 2 copies)
- **mass-spectrometry-spectral-embedding-learning**: Use when when you have preprocessed MS/MS spectral pairs (peak intensities and m/z values) and need to predict molecular structural similarity scores, or when you want to project spectra into a learned chemical embedding space for visualization (e.g., via UMAP) or downstream similarity searches. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/mass-spectrometry-spectral-embedding-learning) · 2 copies)
- **mass-spectrometry-spectral-preprocessing**: Use when you have raw mass spectrometry spectra from an unknown analyte or a synthetic compound library and need to feed them into PS2MS or similar deep learning classifiers for NPS detection. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/mass-spectrometry-spectral-preprocessing) · 2 copies)
- **mass-spectrometry-spectrum-archival-schema-design**: Use when a laboratory needs a central relational database schema to store, link, and archive mass spectra, peptide identifications, and quantitative results from MS-driven proteomics experiments. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-proteomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-proteomics/tree/HEAD/collections/proteomics/v1/leaves/mass-spectrometry-spectrum-archival-schema-design))
- **mass-spectrometry-spectrum-preprocessing**: Use when when you have raw LC–QTOF wastewater spectra (or other real experimental mass spectrometry data) and need to feed them into a pretrained MSGO model for molecular structure prediction. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/mass-spectrometry-spectrum-preprocessing) · 2 copies)
- **mass-spectrometry-spectrum-preprocessing**: Use when raw MS/MS peak files (e.g. mzML) must be converted into fixed-dimensional feature encodings/embeddings for downstream clustering or comparison of millions of spectra, following the GLEAMS learned-embedding approach. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/mass-spectrometry-spectrum-preprocessing--c8552817647b5a01))
- **mass-spectrometry-spectrum-preprocessing**: Use when raw MS/MS spectra (e.g. from an .mgf file like iPRG2012.mgf) must be cleaned and standardized before downstream analysis such as library searching or deep-neural-network processing, and peak lists need mz-range restriction, precursor/noise removal, and intensity scaling. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/mass-spectrometry-spectrum-preprocessing--99e25f8cc3a2c48f))
- **mass-spectrometry-spectrum-pruning**: Decision support for cleaning a large, multi-source mass spectral library (e.g. .MSP, .MGF, .JSON, . ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/mass-spectrometry-spectrum-pruning))
- **mass-spectrometry-spectrum-quality-filtering**: Use when compiling or harmonizing MS/MS spectral libraries from multiple source repositories and you need to identify and remove spectra that fail quality thresholds (low resolution, precursor-fragment mass inconsistency, duplicate fragment patterns, noise-dominated, or missing critical metadata. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/mass-spectrometry-spectrum-quality-filtering) · 2 copies)
- **mass-spectrometry-spectrum-retrieval**: Use when you need to programmatically fetch a specific MS/MS spectrum from a public metabolomics or proteomics repository and you know (or can construct) its USI — e.g. to feed a peak list into a downstream scoring or visualization pipeline. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/mass-spectrometry-spectrum-retrieval))
- **mass-spectrometry-spectrum-tokenization**: Use when you have pre-processed MS/MS spectra and need to prepare them for word-embedding-based similarity methods (e.g., Spec2Vec). ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/mass-spectrometry-spectrum-tokenization) · 4 copies)
- **mass-spectrometry-spectrum-vectorization**: Use when you have high-resolution MS/MS spectra in mzML, mzXML, or MGF format and need to cluster or search across millions of spectra. The vectorization step is necessary before constructing nearest-neighbor indexes or computing pairwise distance matrices for spectrum clustering. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/mass-spectrometry-spectrum-vectorization) · 2 copies)
- **mass-spectrometry-structural-annotation**: Use when after identifying statistically significant features (e. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/mass-spectrometry-structural-annotation) · 3 copies)
- **mass-spectrometry-structure-elucidation-benchmarking**: Use when runtime benchmarking a GPU-accelerated DP structure-elucidation pipeline against a CPU DP baseline on natural-product testing datasets (e.g. Cuscuta chinensis, Ophiopogon japonicus, Polygonum multiflorum, Angelica sp.) at five decimal digits of mass. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/mass-spectrometry-structure-elucidation-benchmarking))
- **mass-spectrometry-substructure-assignment**: Use when after Mass2Motif discovery via LDA on preprocessed MS/MS spectra, when you have a set of inferred motifs (fragments and neutral losses with LDA probabilities) and need to assign putative substructure identities rather than retain anonymous motif labels. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/mass-spectrometry-substructure-assignment) · 2 copies)
- **mass-spectrometry-substructure-mapping**: Use when a GNPS molecular network (classical or feature-based) already exists and MS2LDA motif-document probability/overlap relations must be attached to nodes and edges for substructural visualization. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/mass-spectrometry-substructure-mapping))
- **mass-spectrometry-table-export**: Use when you need per-scan base peak intensity and m/z across MS1-level scans of a Thermo raw file, e.g. to build a base-peak table for instrument QC or TIC-like diagnostics from rawR-readable Orbitrap data. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/mass-spectrometry-table-export))
- **mass-spectrometry-target-list-preparation**: Use when you have a raw .xlsx or tabular file listing candidate compounds with theoretical or measured m/z values, expected retention times, and ionization polarities, and you need to feed this into TARDIS or another targeted LC–MS analysis pipeline. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/mass-spectrometry-target-list-preparation) · 2 copies)
- **mass-spectrometry-tic-filtering**: Use when raw mass spectra have variable total ion counts and ragged, non-uniform m/z grids, and you need a TIC-filtered, uniformly binned feature matrix (CSV) for CNN/Transformer classifiers. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/mass-spectrometry-tic-filtering))
- **mass-spectrometry-tolerance-calibration**: Use when after generating a feature table from mzML data (via Asari) and before performing MS1 or MS2 annotation. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/mass-spectrometry-tolerance-calibration) · 3 copies)
- **mass-spectrometry-tolerance-configuration**: Decision support for setting mass and RT matching tolerances when linking peak-picked features to in silico lipid precursor and fragment libraries in LipidMatch. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/mass-spectrometry-tolerance-configuration))
- **mass-spectrometry-tolerance-window-calibration**: Use when after feature extraction from raw LC-MS or GC-MS data (using XCMS, MS-Dial, or similar), when you have a feature intensity table with m/z and RT metadata and a reference compound database (known molecules list with m/z, RT, and annotation metadata), and you need to assign confidence-ranked. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/mass-spectrometry-tolerance-window-calibration) · 3 copies)
- **mass-spectrometry-transient-signal-simulation**: Use when no experimentally acquired ground-truth high-resolution transients exist to benchmark reconstruction, and you need simulated MSI data with known peak centroids, adduct identities, and tissue-region assignments to quantify SNR gain and correlation fidelity. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v3/leaves/mass-spectrometry-transient-signal-simulation))
- **mass-spectrometry-transition-data-formatting**: Use when when you have raw mass spectrometry transition data from a triple-quadrupole or other tandem MS instrument and need to prepare it for suspect chemical screening using EISA-EXPOSOME, or when merging custom compound libraries into the T3DB format. ([HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics](https://github.com/HolobiomicsLab/asb-skills-metabolomics/tree/HEAD/collections/metabolomics/v2/leaves/mass-spectrometry-transition-data-formatting) · 2 copies)
- **mass-spectrometry-transition-pair-counting**: Use when quantifying how fast a targeted-lipidomics tool enumerates precursor-product ion pairs for a transition list, or when reproducing LipidCreator's 60,000 pairs/s benchmark. - **mass-spectrum-conditional-molecule-generation**: Use when you have a tandem mass spectrum plus an inferred molecular formula and need candidate molecular structures ranked by top-k accuracy, MCES distance, or Tanimoto similarity against a ground-truth molecule. - **mass-to-charge-tolerance-matching**: Use when you have statistically significant LC-MS features and need to group them into structural clusters. - **mass-tolerance-calibration-ppm-units**: Use when when linking statistically significant LC-MS features into structural clusters based on adduct signatures and cross-assay references (e.g., [M+H]+/[M-H]−), and you need to specify the maximum allowed deviation (in ppm) between observed m/z values and calculated neutral masses. - **mass-tolerance-parameterization**: Use when constraining a Pickaxe network expansion to an unannotated metabolomics peak list and you must choose the adduct list and user-defined mass tolerance that decide which predicted compounds are retained. ([massgen/MassGen](https://github.com/massgen/MassGen/tree/HEAD/massgen/skills/massgen) · 4 copies)
- **massgen-config-creator**: Guide for creating properly structured YAML configuration files for MassGen. This skill should be used when agents need to create new configs for examples, case studies, testing, or demonstrating features. ([massgen/MassGen](https://github.com/massgen/MassGen/tree/HEAD/massgen/skills/massgen-config-creator))
- **massgen-develops-massgen**: Guide for using MassGen to develop and improve itself. This skill should be used when agents need to run MassGen experiments programmatically (using automation mode) OR analyze terminal UI/UX quality (using visual evaluation tools). These are mutually exclusive workflows for different improvement goals. ([massgen/MassGen](https://github.com/massgen/MassGen/tree/HEAD/massgen/skills/massgen-develops-massgen))
- **massgen-log-analyzer**: Run MassGen experiments and analyze logs using automation mode, logfire tracing, and SQL queries. Use this skill for performance analysis, debugging agent behavior, evaluating coordination patterns, and improving the logging structure, or whenever an ANALYSIS_REPORT.md is needed in a log directory. ([massgen/MassGen](https://github.com/massgen/MassGen/tree/HEAD/massgen/skills/massgen-log-analyzer))
- **massgen-release-documenter**: Guide for following MassGen's release documentation workflow. This skill should be used when preparing release documentation, updating changelogs, writing case studies, or maintaining project documentation across releases. ([massgen/MassGen](https://github.com/massgen/MassGen/tree/HEAD/massgen/skills/massgen-release-documenter))
- **massgen-run**: Launch a MassGen multi-agent run. Multiple LLM backends (codex, gemini, claude, grok) collaborate on a task via voting and consensus. Runs in Docker containers by default. ([massgen/massgen-refinery](https://github.com/massgen/massgen-refinery/tree/HEAD/skills/massgen-run) · ★1)
- **massgen-serena**: This skill provides symbol-level code understanding and navigation using Language Server Protocol (LSP). Enables IDE-like capabilities for finding symbols, tracking references, and making precise code edits at the symbol level. ([Alternative-Function-Four/AFF](https://github.com/Alternative-Function-Four/AFF/tree/HEAD/.agents/skills/massgen-serena))
